((1. nūṉmarapu))

iv= ōttu eṉ nutaliṟṟu* ō ~eṉiṉ, atu ~um ataṉ peyar urippa ~ē aṭaṅkum. iv= atikārattāṟ collappaṭum eḻuttu*-ilakkaṇattiṉi ōrāṟṟāl tokuttu uṇarttutaliṉ, nūṉmarapu eṉṉum peyarttu. itaṉuḷ+ kūṟukiṉṟa ilakkaṇam moḻi ~iṭi (niṉṟa) eḻuttiṟku* aṉṟi+ taṉi-niṉṟa eḻuttiṟku* eṉa uṇarka.

((1. eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vaki))

TE1i

{Z 1}

{C TOL}
eḻuttu* eṉappaṭupa
akara mutal ṉakara iṟuvāy
muppaḵtu* eṉpa
cārntu-varal marapiṉ mūṉṟu* alaṅkaṭi ~ē.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} i+ taliccūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟu* ō ~eṉiṉ eḻuttukkaḷatu peyar -um muṟi -~um toki -~um uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}eḻuttu eṉappaṭupa{/T}
eḻuttu* eṉṟu ciṟappittu+ collappaṭuvaṉa
{T}akaram mutal ṉakara iṟuvāy muppaḵtu eṉpa{/T}
akaram ākiya mutali ~uṭiyaṉa -~um ṉakaram ākiya iṟuvāyiṉi ~uṭiyaṉa -~um ākiya muppatu* eṉṟu collupa (āciriyar)
{T}cārntu -varal marapiṉ mūṉṟu alaṅkaṭi{/T}
cārntu varutal ākiya ilakkaṇattiṉi ~uṭiya mūṉṟu* um allāviṭattu.

mūṉṟu* -um āṉa -~iṭattu muppattu-mūṉṟu eṉṟu collupa eṉṟavāṟu.

e-ṭu: a-ā-i-ī-u-ū-e-ē-ai-o-ō-au; k-ṅ-c-ñ-ṭ-ṇ-t-n-p-m-y-r-l-v-ḻ-ḷ-ṟ-ṉ eṉa varum.

{T}eṉappaṭupa{/T} eṉṟa ciṟappāṉ, aḷapeṭi -~um uyirmey= -um varivaṭivu* -um ciṟappu* illā eḻuttu* āka+ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. a ā eṉpaṉa peyar: muṟi, a+ muṟi; toki muppatu; avaṟṟuḷ, akaram tāṉ -um iyaṅki+ taṉimeykaḷi iyakkutal+ ciṟappāṉ, muṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. ṉakāram vīṭu-pēṟṟiṟku* uriya āṇ-pāli uṇarttutal+ ciṟappāṉ piṉ vikka+-paṭṭatu.

toki (~eṉpatu) tokiyuḷtoki -~um, tokiyuḷvaki -~um, tokiyuḷviri -~um, vakiyuḷtoki -~um, vakiyuḷvaki -~um, vakiyuḷviri -~um, viriyuḷtoki -~um, viriyuḷvaki -~um, viriyuḷviri -~um eṉa oṉpatu vakippaṭum.

eḻuttu* eṉpatu tokiyuḷtoki, muppatu* eṉpatu ataṉ vaki. muppattumūṉṟu* eṉpatu ataṉ viri. muppatu* eṉpatu vakiyuḷtoki. muppattumūṉṟu* eṉpatu ataṉ vaki. aḷapeṭi talippeytu nāṟpatu* eṉpatu ataṉ viri. muppattumūṉṟu* eṉpatu viriyuḷtoki. nāṟpatu* eṉpatu ataṉ vaki. uyirmey talippeytu irunūṟṟimpattāṟu* eṉpatu ataṉ viri. ceyyuḷiṉpam nōkki vakaram nīkki+ pakaram iṭappaṭṭatu. akara mutal ṉakara ~iṟuvāy eṉṉa, irupeyaroṭṭu* ākupeyarāṉ muppataṉ mēl niṉṟaṉa.

"taṉṉiṉa muṭittal" eṉpataṉāl, ēkāram um yakara ākāram um viṉā+ peṟumeṉa+ koḷka.

i+ kuṟikaḷi ~um muṉ kuṟil eṉṟu* um neṭil eṉṟu* um kūṟiyavaḻi ~ē kūṟuka ~eṉiṉ, ivi col nilimiyiṟ peṟum kuṟi ~ākaliṉ, āṇṭu viyātu moḻimarapiṉi+ cāra vittār eṉka. i+ kuṟi moḻinilimikkēl eḻuttiṉmēl vittu+ kūṟiyatu eṉṉi ~eṉiṉ, iv= atikārattu+ peyar viṉi ~allaṉavaṟṟiṟku+ karuvi ceyyāmiyiṉ eṉka.

{T}uḷa ~eṉa moḻipa eṉmaṉār pulavar{/T}

((mutal āvatu nūṉmarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.))

((2. moḻimarapu))

iv= ōttu eṉṉa peyarttu* -ō ~eṉiṉ, moḻikaḷukku eḻuttāṉ varum marapu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ moḻi-marapu eṉa+-paṭṭatu. itaṉ -uḷ kūṟukiṉṟatu taṉi-niṉṟa eḻuttiṟku* aṉṟi moḻi-~iṭi (niṉṟa) eḻuttiṟku* eṉa ~uṇarka.

{T}iraṇṭu iṟantu icikkum toṭarmoḻi{/T}

{T}uḷappaṭa mūṉṟu{/T}

{T}tōṉṟiya neṟi{/T}

e-ṭu: {{ā, maṇi, varaku, koṟṟaṉ}} eṉa varum.

((4. eḻuttukkaḷiṉ iyakkam))

{{ñaḻiyiṟṟu}} eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa vilakkiṉa ~um varum āl eṉiṉ, avi aḻivaḻakku* eṉṟu maṟukka.

iṉi {T}ēṉa{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, mutal ām eṉṉappaṭṭa oṉpatu uyirmey= um paṉṉiraṇṭu uyir um tam peyar kūṟumvaḻi ~um moḻikku mutal ām eṉa+ koḷka.

((iraṇṭāvatu moḻimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.))

{U 3} {Z 0}

((3. piṟappiyal))

((eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappilakkaṇam uṇarttuvatu))

iv= ōttu eṉṉa peyarttu* ō ~eṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ piṟappiyal eṉṉum peyarttu. itaṉi nūṉmarapiṉ piṉ= ē vikka ~eṉiṉ, cārpiṟṟōṟṟatteḻuttu* um taṉimey= um moḻimarapiṭi uṇartti+ [piṟappu] uṇartta vēṇṭutaliṉ, moḻimarapiṉ piṉṉatu* āyiṟṟu.

{^L}

((1. eḻuttukkaḷ potuvāka+ piṟakkumāṟu))

TE83i

{Z 1}

{C TOL}
unti mutalā muntu vaḷi tōṉṟi+
taliyiṉ um miṭaṟṟiṉ um neñciṉ um nilii+
pal= um itaḻ um nā ~um mūkku* um
aṇṇam um uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟi niliyāṉ
uṟuppu* uṟṟu* amiya neṟippaṭa nāṭi
ellā ~eḻuttu* um^ collum^ kāli+
piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟuvēṟu* iyala
tiṟappaṭa+ teriyum^ kāṭciyāṉa.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} i+ taliccūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟō ~eṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu potu+ piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}ellā eḻuttu* um neṟippaṭa nāṭi collum kāli{/T}
tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ ellām oruvaṉ muṟippaṭa ārāyntu tammi+ colluṅkālattu
{T}unti mutalā muntu vaḷi tōṉṟi{/T}
koppūḻ aṭiyāka mēl ē kiḷarkiṉṟa utāṉaṉ eṉṉum peyari ~uṭiya vāyu+ tōṉṟi
{T}taliyiṉ um miṭaṟṟiṉ um neñciṉ um nilii{/T}
taliyiṉkaṇ= um miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇ= um neñciṉkaṇ= um nilipeṟṟu

(tali ~um miṭaṟu* um neñcu* um eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉōṭu* um) pal= um itaḻ um nā~ um mūkku* um aṇṇam um uḷappaṭa eṭṭu* ākiya muṟimiyi ~uṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil ōr uṟuppōṭu ōr uṟuppu+ tammiṟ porunti amitipeṟa
{T}piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟu vēṟu iyala{/T}

kūṟupāṭu* uḷatu* āka ārāyum aṟiviṭattu.

((4. puṇariyal))

((eḻuttukkaḷiṉ puṇarcci ~ilakkaṇam uṇarttuvatu))

iv= ōttu eṉṉa peyarttu* ō ~eṉiṉ, moḻikaḷ, (mēl+ ceykiyōttukkaḷ uḷ) puṇartaṟku* uriya karuviyiṉ iyalpu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ puṇariyal eṉappaṭṭatu. mēl moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya moḻikaḷ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ moḻimarapiṉōṭu iyipu* uṭittu* āyiṟṟu.

{Z 1}

((1. moḻikaḷiṉ mutal um īṟu* um))

((moḻikku mutalil um iṟutiyil um varum eḻuttukkaḷ))

{C TOL}
mūṉṟu taliyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟu* eḻuttiṉ
iraṇṭu taliyiṭṭa mutalāku* irupaḵtu
aṟunāṉku* īṟṟoṭu neṟi niṉṟu* iyalum
ellā moḻikku* um iṟuti ~um mutal um
mey= ē ~uyir eṉṟu āy īr iyala.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} i+ tali+ cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟu* ō ~eṉiṉ, moḻimarapiṉuḷ mutal um īṟu* um kūṟiyavaḻi uyir um mey= um uyirmey= um eṉa mūṉṟu* āy virintuniṉṟataṉi uyir um mey= um eṉa iraṇṭu* āka+ tokuttalāṉ um, avvaḻi irupattiraṇṭu eḻuttu moḻikku mutal, irupattunāṉku eḻuttu moḻikku īṟu eṉa+ kūṟaliṉ, muppattumūṉṟu eṉappaṭṭa eḻuttu nāṟpattāṟu āvaṉa pōla virintataṉi avi ~ellām muppattumūṉṟiṉ uḷḷaṉa ~ē eṉa+ tokuttalāṉ um virintatu tokuttal nutaliṟṟu.

e-ṭu. {{maram, ili, āl, viḷa}} eṉa mey= um uyir um, mutal um īṟu* um āyiṉa.

irupattiraṇṭu eḻuttu mutal āvaṉa paṉṉiraṇṭu uyir um oṉpatu uyirmey= um moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram um eṉa ivi. irupattunāṉku eḻuttu īṟu* āvaṉa paṉṉiraṇṭu uyir um patiṉoru puḷḷi ~um īṟṟu+ kuṟṟiyalukaram um eṉa ivi. `īṟṟoṭu' eṉpatu vikārattāṟ ^tokkatu. mey mutaṟ kūṟiyavataṉāl, nālvaki+ puṇarcci ~um meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu uyirkkaṇ nikaḻātu* eṉpatu koḷka.

{T}olvaḻi oṟṟu iṭi mikutal vēṇṭum{/T}

e-ṭu: {{ūrkku, nīrkku, ūrkkaṇ nīrkkaṇ}} eṉa valleḻuttu mikkaṉa. {{taṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ}} eṉa melleḻuttu mikkaṉa.

`olvaḻi' eṉṟataṉāl, {{aracarkaṇ, pārppārkaṇ}} eṉa oṟṟu mikātaṉa ~um koḷka. "meypiṟitātal" (([E110i])) mutalāya nāṉku puṇarcci ~um urupu puṇarccikkaṇ= um eytaliṉ, meypiṟitu* ātali eṭuttōtātu mikkataṉi eṭuttōtiyavataṉāṉ, mikka puṇarcci ~allaṉa ~um īṇṭu* ē koḷka. {{poṟku, poṟkaṇ, āṅkaṇ, īṅkaṇ, ūṅkaṇ, avaṟku, ivaṟku, nuṅkaṇ, koṟṟaṟku, cāttaṟku}} eṉa varum.

{{avaṉ kaṇ, cāttaṉkaṇ}} eṉpuḻi iyalpu* um itaṉāṉ ē koḷka.

(([ANONYME]))

oṭṭutaṟku* oḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi+
col+ citar maruṅkiṉ vaḻi vantu viḷaṅkātu
iṭi niṉṟu* iyalum^ cāriyi iyaṟki
uṭimi ~um iṉmi ~um oṭu vayiṉ okkum.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, cāriyikaṭku* ellām potu ~āyatu* ōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}peyarum toḻilum pirintu oruṅku icippa{/T}
peyarccol= um viṉiccol= um peyar um viṉi ~um āy+ pirintu* um peyar um peyar um āy+ kūṭi ~um icippa
{T}vēṟṟumi urupu nili peṟu vaḻi ~um{/T}
vēṟṟumi ~urupu tokātu nili peṟṟa iṭattiṉ um
{T}tōṟṟam vēṇṭā+ tokutikkaṇ= um{/T}
avi tōṟṟutal vēṇṭāta tokutikkaṇ= um
{T}oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi{/T}
tām tām poruntutaṟku* ēṟpa naṭanta vaḻakkōṭu porunti
{T}col citar maruṅkiṉ{/T}
cāriyi peṟum puṇarmoḻikaḷi+ pirittu+ kāṇumiṭattu
{T}vaḻi vantu viḷaṅkātu iṭiniṉṟu iyalum{/T}
avaṟṟiṉ piṉ vantu viḷaṅkātu avaṟṟu* iṭi ~ē niṉṟu naṭakkum
{T}cāriyi iyaṟki{/T}
cāriyiyiṉ iyalpu
{T}uṭimi ~um iṉmi ~um oṭu vayiṉ okkum{/T}
avi tām uṇṭātal um illiyātal um oṭu urupiṉiṭattu okkum.

e-ṭu: {{viḷaviṉi+ kuṟittāṉ, viḷaviṉi+ kuṟittavaṉ}} eṉa ~um, {{nilāttu+ koṇṭāṉ, nilāttu+ koṇṭavaṉ}} eṉa ~um varum.

{T}oṭṭutaṟku* oḻukiya{/T} vaḻakku* aṉmiyiṉ, {{nilā+ katir nilā muṟṟam}} eṉpaṉa cāriyi peṟā ~āyiṉa.

{{ellānammiyum}} eṉa+ cāriyi īṟṟiṉkaṇ= um varutaliṉ, {T}iṭi niṉṟu* iyaṟal{/T} perumpāṉmi ~eṉakkoḷka.

{{pūviṉoṭu virinta kūntal}} eṉa ~um, {{pūvoṭu virinta kūntal}} eṉa ~um, {T}uṭimi ~um iṉmi ~um oṭuvayiṉ{/T} ottavāṟu.

{T}iyaṟki{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ oṭu ~urupiṉkaṇ cāriyi peṟutal um peṟāmi ~um oḻiya, orōvaḻi+ peṟṟu* ē varum eṉpatu koḷka. palavaṟṟōṭu eṉa varum.

{{avaṟṟu+ kōṭu}} eṉa varum. iḵtu aikāra īṟu.

īṇṭu {T}valleḻuttu mikum{/T} eṉṟatu, īṟṟu_val=-eḻuttu* iṉṟi+ tirintu muṭiyum ṉakaram -um ṇakaram -um lakaram -um ḷakaram -um eṉa ivaṟṟi nōkki ~eṉa ~uṇarka.

{T}uyir mika varuvaḻi uyir keṭa varutal um{/T}
uyir mika varumiṭattu av= uyir keṭa varutal um
{T}cāriyi uḷvaḻi cāriyi keṭutal um{/T}
cāriyi ~uḷḷa iṭattu+ cāriyi keṭutal um
{T}cāriyi uḷvaḻi taṉ urupu niliyal um{/T}
cāriyi uḷḷaviṭattu+ cāriyiyoṭu taṉ= urupu niṟṟal um
{T}cāriyi iyaṟki uṟaḻa+ tōṉṟal um{/T}
cāriyi peṟuka ~eṉṟa vaḻi+ cāriyi peṟātu iyalpu* ākiya moḻikaḷ varumoḻiyiṉ um nilimoḻiyiṉ um mikku* um tirintu* um varumoḻi valleḻuttukkaḷ uṟaḻcci ~āka+ tōṉṟutal um
{T}uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutal um{/T}
uyartiṇi+ peyariṭattu+ taṉ= urupu tokātu* ē virintu varutal um
{T}aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyarkku a iyal niliyal um{/T}
uyartiṇiyōṭu aḵṟiṇi viravu+ peyarkku* um av= urupu av= iyalpil ē niṟṟal um
{T}mey piṟatu āku iṭattu iyaṟki ātal um{/T}
mey piṟitu* āy muṭiyumiṭattu iyalpu* āy muṭital um
{T}aṉṉa piṟa ~um{/T}
attaṉmiyaṉa piṟa muṭipukaḷ um
{T}taṉ iyal maruṅkiṉ meypeṟa kiḷantu poruḷ varintu icikkum{/T}
taṉatu iyalpu* ākiya kūṟṟāl akattōttiṉuḷ+ poruḷpeṟa eṭuttōtappaṭṭa vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarcciyatu potu muṭiviṉi+ tāṉ varintu vēṟu muṭipiṟṟu* āy niṉṟu olikkum
{T}aikāra vēṟṟumi+ tiripu eṉa moḻipa{/T}
aikāra vēṟṟumiyiṉatu vēṟupāṭṭu+ puṇarcci eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.

e-ṭu: {{viḷakkuṟittāṉ}} eṉpatu melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valleḻuttu mikkatu. {{maraṅkuṟittāṉ}} eṉpatu valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melleḻuttu mikkatu. {{tāykkoli}} eṉpatu iyalpu* āmiṭattu mikkatu. {{palākkuṟittāṉ}} eṉpatu uyir mika varuvaḻi uyir keṭṭatu. {{vaṇṭukoṇarntāṉ}} eṉpatu cāriyi uḷvaḻi+ cāriyi keṭṭatu. {{vaṇṭiṉikkoṇarntāṉ}} eṉpatu cāriyi uḷvaḻi+ taṉ= urupu niliyiṟṟu. {{puḷikuṟittāṉ, puḷikkuṟittāṉ, pūlkuṟittāṉ, pūṟkuṟittāṉ}} eṉa ivi cāriyi ~iyaṟki uṟaḻa+ tōṉṟiṉa. {{nampiyikkoṇarntāṉ}} eṉpatu uyartiṇimaruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu vantatu. {{koṟṟaṉikkoṇarntāṉ}} eṉpatu viravuppeyarkku av= iyal niliyatu. {{maṇkoṇarntāṉ}} eṉpatu mey piṟitu* ākiṭattu iyaṟki ~āy vantatu.

aṉṉa piṟa ~um eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷvaṉa {{kaḻikuṟittāṉ, tiṉipiḷantāṉ}} eṉpaṉa. piṟa ~um aṉṉa.

oḻiyātu eṉṟataṉāṉ orōvaḻi oḻintu* um varum.

{{avaṟkaṇṭu}} eṉa ~um,
«oṉṉārtteṟalum»
(([kuṟaḷ:264]))

eṉa ~um ivi uyartiṇiyuḷ oḻintu vantaṉa. {{makaṟpeṟṟāṉ makaṭpeṟṟāṉ}} eṉa ivi viravu+ peyaruḷ oḻintu vantaṉa.

iv= ilēcu taṉṉāṉ ē, ivaṟṟiṉ muṭipu vēṟṟumi ~um koḷka.

{T}meypeṟa{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, uṟaḻcci ~āy muṭivaṉa ~um koḷka.

{{mikoṇarntāṉ, mikkoṇarntāṉ}} eṉa ~um; {{vilkōḷ, viṟkōḷ}} eṉa ~um varum. ivvāṟu tirintu muṭiyātu, akattōttil potumuṭipu* ē muṭipu* āy+ {{kaṭukkuṟittāṉ, ceppukkoṇarntāṉ}} eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa muṭivaṉa aṟintu koḷka. ivvāṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭivatu perumpāṉmi ~um iraṇṭāvatu viṉiyōṭu muṭiyumvaḻi+ pōlum. atu {T}tammiṉ ākiya toḻiṟcol% muṉ variṉ{/T} (([E157i])) eṉa niṉṟa atikārattāṟ koḷḷavittār pōlum. "taṉṉiṉamuṭittal" eṉpataṉāṉ, ēḻām vēṟṟumi viṉiyoṭu muṭiyumvaḻi+ tiripum koḷka.
«vari pāy varuṭi»,
(([mali:503]))
«pulam pukkaṉaṉ ē pul= aṇaṟ kāḷi»
(([puṟam:258_6]))

eṉa varum.

{T}oru peyar{/T} eṉṟatu, niṉ eṉpatu* um vēṟoru peyar pōṟali vilakkiṟṟu. `peyar kuṟukum' eṉṉātu {T}mutal kuṟukum{/T} eṉṟatu, a+ peyariṉ neṭṭeḻuttu niliyatu a+ kuṟukkam eṉṟaṟku* eṉpatu. {T}neṭumutal{/T} eṉṟatu a+ moḻimutaliṉ nakaram kuṟukutali vilakkiṟṟu. īṇṭu, uyirmey ~oṟṟumipaṟṟi neṭiyatu mutal āyiṟṟu.

cāriyi+ pēṟṟiṭi eḻuttu+ pēṟu kūṟiyatu, mūṉṟām urupiṉkaṇ cāriyi peṟṟu* ē vanta atikāram māṟṟi niṉṟatu.

((ōkāra īṟṟukku oṉ cāriyi))

TE181i

{Z 8}

{C TOL}

{WFW}

aṉṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum uvama urupu* ākiya iṭiccol= um
{T}aṇmi cuṭṭiya viḷinili+ kiḷavi ~um{/T}
aṇiyāri+ karutiya viḷi ~ākiya nilimiyi ~uṭiya uyartiṇi+ peyarccol= um
{T}ceymmaṉa eṉṉum toḻil iṟu col= um{/T}
ceymmaṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum viṉiccol= ākiya akaravīṟṟu+ col= um
{T}ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷ kiḷavi ~um{/T}
ēvali+ karutiya viyaṅkōḷ ākiya viṉiccol= um
{T}ceyta eṉṉum peyar-eñcu-kiḷavi ~um{/T}
ceyta eṉṟu collappaṭum peyareccam ākiya viṉiccol= um
{T}ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉi eñcu kiḷavi ~um{/T}
ceyyiya eṉṟu collappaṭum viṉiyeccam ākiya viṉiccol= um
{T}amma eṉṉum uripporuḷ kiḷavi ~um{/T}
amma eṉṟu collappaṭum uriyaci+ poruṇmiyi ~uṭiya iṭiccol= um
{T}palavaṟṟu iṟuti+ peyarkkoṭi uḷappaṭa aṉṟi aṉittum{/T}
paṉmi+ poruḷkaḷiṉ akara ~īṟṟu+ pala ~eṉṉum peyarccol= um ākiya av= aṉittu+ col= um
{T}iyalpu eṉa moḻipa{/T}
iyalpu* āy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar (āciriyar.)

e-ṭu: {{poṉ aṉṉa kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi}} eṉa ~um; {{ūra koḷ, cel, tā, pō}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇmaṉa kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi}} eṉa ~um; {{celka kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇṭa kutiri, cennāy takar, paṉṟi}} eṉa ~um; itaṉ etirmaṟi {{uṇṇāta kutiri}} eṉa ~um; itaṉ kuṟippu {{nalla kutiri, cennāy}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇṇiya koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ}} eṉa ~um; {{amma koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā}} eṉa ~um; {{pala kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi}} eṉa ~um varum.

{{ūra koḷ}} eṉpatu uyirīṟu* ākiya uyartiṇippeyar eṉpataṉ uḷ aṭaṅkātu* ō ~eṉiṉ, {{ūrāṉ}} eṉpatu {{ūra}} eṉa īṟu tirintamiyiṉ āṇṭu aṭaṅkātu* āyiṟṟu* eṉpatu.

{T}viḷinilikkiḷavi{/T} ~ākiya peyari muṉ vittataṉāl, ceyta ~eṉpataṉ maṟi ~aṉṟi+ ceyyum eṉpataṟku maṟi ~ākiya ceyyāta eṉpatu* um av= iyalpu uṭittu* eṉa+ koḷka. atu {{vārāta koṟṟaṉ}} eṉa varum.

`uriyiṟ kōṭal' eṉpataṉāl, viyaṅkōḷ muṉ vikkal+ pālataṉi muṉ viyātu ceymmaṉa eṉpataṉi muṉ vittataṉāṉ, iv= iyalpu muṭipiṉkaṇ ceymmaṉa eṉpatu ciṟappu* uṭittu* eṉa+ peṟappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl, `ēvalkaṇṇāta viyaṅkōḷ um' iv= iyalpu-muṭipu uṭittu* eṉa+ koḷka. atu,
«maṉṉiya peruma nī»
(([puṟam:6_29]))

eṉa varum.

{T}palavaṟṟu* iṟuti+ peyar+ koṭi{/T} eṉpataṉi amma eṉpataṟku muṉ viyātataṉāl, pala eṉpatu mēṟkūṟum ceyyuḷ muṭipil tirintu muṭital īṇṭi iyalpiṟ ciṟappiṉṟu* eṉpatūum, akaravīṟṟuḷ muṭipu kūṟāta muṟṟuviṉi ~um viṉikkuṟippu* um av= iyalpiṉa ~eṉpatūum peṟappaṭum. {{uṇṭaṉa kutiri}} eṉpatu muṟṟuviṉi. {{kariya kutiri}} eṉpatu muṟṟuviṉikkuṟippu.

`taṉṉiṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāl, pala eṉpatiṉ iṉam ākiya cila eṉpataṟku* um av= iyalpu koḷka. {{cila kutiri}} eṉa varum.

((`vāḻiya' eṉṉum col))

TE212i

{Z 9}

{C TOL}
vāḻiya ~eṉṉum^ cēy eṉ kiḷavi
iṟuti yakaram^ keṭutal um urittu* ē.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, ēvalkaṇṇāta viyaṅkōṭkaḷil oṉṟaṟku eytiya iyalpu vilakki vikāram kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}vāḻiya eṉṉum cēy eṉ kiḷavi{/T}
vāḻiya eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa avvāḻuṅkālam aṇmiya ~aṉṟi cēymiya eṉṟu uṇarttum col
{T}iṟuti yakaram keṭutal um urittu{/T}
taṉ= iṟutikkaṇ akaram um ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa yakara ~oṟṟu* um keṭātu muṭital ē ~aṉṟi+ keṭṭu muṭital um urittu.

e-ṭu: {{vāḻikoṟṟā}} eṉa varum.

{T}cēy eṉ kiḷavi{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, a+ muṭipu iv `vāḻiya' eṉpataṟku ēval kaṇṇāta niliyatu* eṉpatu viḷakkiya niṉṟatu. "oṉṟeṉa muṭittal" eṉpataṉāl, piṟa kaṇattukkaṇ= um i+ viti koḷka. {{vāḻiñeḷḷā}} eṉa varum.

((`amma' eṉṉum collukku mēlum oruviti))

TE214i

{C TOL}

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, mēl+ {T}palavaṟṟu* iṟuti+ peyar+ koṭi{/T} (([E211i])) eṉṟu ōtiyataṟku+ ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}palavaṟṟu iṟuti nīṭum moḻi uḷa{/T}
pala eṉṉum colliṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭu muṭiyum moḻikaḷ um uḷa; (yāṇṭu uḷa ~eṉil)
{T}ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻiyāṉa{/T}
ceyyuḷ ātali+ karutiya oṉṟōṭu* oṉṟu toṭarccippaṭum ceyyuḷ muṭipu* uṭiya moḻikaḷiṉ kaṇ= ē.

`ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya moḻi' eṉṉātu {T}toṭarmoḻi{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, {{ippala}} ~eṉpatu nīḷumvaḻi varumoḻi ~āvatu {{cila}} ~eṉpatu* ē eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum. `ceyyuḷāṉa' eṉṉātu {T}ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻiyāṉa{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, {{pala}} eṉṉum moḻi ~īṟu nīṇṭavaḻi nilimoḻi akarappēṟu* um varumoḻi ñakāra melleḻuttuppēṟu* um, varumoḻi ~īṟu nīṇṭavaḻi akarappēṟu* um makaram ākiya melleḻuttu+ pēṟu* um koḷka. `uṇṭu' eṉṉātu {T}uḷa{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, cila ~eṉṉum varumoḻi ~iṟuti nīṭal um koḷka.

(([ANONYME]))

`lakaram ṟakaram ām' eṉṉātu {T}lakaram ṟakara ~oṟṟu* ām{/T} eṉṟa ñāpakattāṉ, akaram keṭum eṉṟāṉ āka+ koḷka. aruttāpatti mukattāl, {T}tam muṉ tām variṉ lakaram ṟakara ~oṟṟu* ām{/T} eṉa ~ē, tam muṉ piṟa vantaviṭattu lakaram ṟakara ~oṟṟu* ākātu akaram keṭum eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. {{palpaṭi, palyāṉi, cilpaṭi, cilvēḷvi}} eṉa varum.

{T}toṭar al iṟuti{/T} eṉpatu cuṭṭu* allatu ōreḻuttorumoḻi akaram iṉmiyiṉ, ōreḻuttorumoḻi mēṟ cellātu* āyiṟṟu. urittu* eṉṟatu, akaravīṟṟu* orumi paṟṟi.

{INTRO} itu, av= īṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku iyalpu kūṟi eytiyatu vilakkutal um eytātatu eytuvittal um nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}ā ~um mā ~um viḷippeyarkkiḷavi ~um{/T}

{T}ēval kuṟitta uriyaci miyā ~um{/T}

{T}taṉ toḻil urikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyoṭu aṉṟi aṉittum{/T}

{T}iyalpu eṉa moḻipa{/T}

e-ṭu: {{ākuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu}} eṉa ~um; {{mākuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu}} eṉa ~um; {{ūrākoḷ, cel, tā, pō}} eṉa ~um varum; {{yākuṟiya, ciṟiya, tīya, periya}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇṇākutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi}} eṉa ~um; {{kēṇmiyākoṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇkā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā}} eṉa ~um varum.

{T}viḷippeyar+ kiḷavi ~um{/T}, {T}palavaṟṟu* iṟuti ~um{/T}, {T}ēval kuṟitta uriyaci miyā ~um{/T}, {T}taṉ ^toḻil urikkum viṉā ~um{/T} eytātatu eytuvikkappaṭṭaṉa. {{ūrā koḷ}} eṉpatu uyir īṟu* ākiya uyartiṇippeyar eṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātu* ō ~eṉiṉ, muṉ (([E211i])) kūṟiṟṟu* ē kūṟuka.

{T}cuṭṭiṉ iyaṟki muṉ kiḷanta aṟṟu{/T}
ikara ~īṟṟu+ cuṭṭiṉatu iyalpu iyalpukaṇam varumvaḻi ~um uyirkkaṇam varumvaḻi ~um muṉ akaravīṟṟu+ cuṭṭiṟku+ collappaṭṭa taṉmittu* ām.

eṉṟatu meṉkaṇam varumvaḻi a+ melleḻuttu mikkum (([E206i])) iṭikkaṇam varumvaḻi ~um uyirkkaṇam varum vaḻi ~um nilimoḻi vakaram peṟṟu* um (([E207i, E208i])) ceyyuṭkaṇ vakaram keṭṭu+ cuṭṭu nīṇṭu* um (([E209i])) muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

e-ṭu: {{iññāṉam, innūl, immaṇi}} eṉa ~um; {{ivyāḻ, ivvaṭṭu}} eṉa ~um; {{ivvaṭi, ivvāṭi, ivvuviyam}} eṉa ~um {{īvayiṉāṉa}} eṉa ~um varum.

((`tūṇi' muṉ `patakku'))

TE240i

{Z 37}

{C TOL}
patakku muṉ variṉ ē tūṇi+ kiḷavi
mutal+ kiḷantu* eṭutta vēṟṟumi ~iyaṟṟu* ē.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= īṟṟu alvaḻikaḷil aḷavuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku mēl toki marapiṉuḷ (([E165i])) eytiya ē eṉ cāriyi vilakki vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}patakku muṉ variṉ tūṇi+ kiḷavi mutal kiḷantu eṭutta vēṟṟumi iyaṟṟu{/T}
patakku eṉṉum col taṉ muṉ variṉ tūṇi eṉṉum collaḷavu muṉpu vitanteṭutta vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉ iyalpiṟṟu* āy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.

e-ṭu: {{tūṇippatakku}} eṉa varum.

varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, {{irutūṇippatakku}} eṉa aṭiyaṭuttu vantavaḻi ~um iv viti koḷka. {T}kiḷanteṭutta{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, tūṇi muṉṉar+ piṟa poruṭpeyar vantavaḻi ~um āṇṭu nilimoḻi ~aṭi ~aṭuttu vantavaḻi ~um taṉ muṉṉar+ tāṉ vantavaḻi ~um i+ muṭipu koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉ ē, taṉ muṉṉar+ tāṉ vantavaḻi ikku+ cāriyi+ pēṟu* um koḷka.

e-ṭu: {{tūṇikkoḷ, tūṇiccāmi}} eṉa ~um; {{iru tūṇi+ koḷ}} eṉa ~um; {{tūṇittūṇi}} eṉa ~um; {{tūṇikkuttūṇi}} eṉa ~um varum.

((`nāḻi' muṉ `uri'))

{C TOL}

e-ṭu: nāṭuri eṉa varum.

varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, nilimoḻi aṭi ~aṭuttu {{irunāṭuri}} eṉṟa vaḻi ~um i+ muṭipu koḷka. `ikaram' eṉṉātu `iṟuti ikaram' eṉṟataṉāl īṇṭi nilimoḻi ~um varumoḻi ~um nilimoḻikaḷ āy niṉṟu piṟa poruṭpeyarōṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum koḷka. {{nāḻikkāyam, urikkāyam}} eṉa varum.

aṉṟu varu kāli ā ~ākutal um
ai varu kāli mey varintu keṭutal um
ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ urittu* eṉa moḻipa.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= īṟṟu+ cuṭṭu mutal+ peyarkku ōr ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}aṉṟu varukāli ā ākutal um{/T}
atikārattāṉ niṉṟa cuṭṭu mutal ukara ~īṟṟu+ peyar aṉṟu eṉṉum col varumoḻi ~āy varuṅkālattu av= ukaram ākāram āki+ tirintu muṭital um
{T}ai varukāli mey varintu keṭutal um{/T}
ai eṉṉum cāriyi iṭivantu muṭiyuṅkālattu av= ukaram tāṉ ūrnta meyyi oḻittu+ keṭṭu muṭital um
{T}ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ urittu eṉa moḻipa{/T}
av= iru muṭipu* um ceyyuṭkaṇ urittu* eṉṟu colluvar.

e-ṭu: {{atāaṉṟamma, itāaṉṟamma, utāaṉṟamma}} eṉa ~um; {{atimaṟṟamma, itimaṟṟamma, utimaṟṟamma}} eṉa ~um varum.

((vēṟṟumiyiṉ ukara īṟṟu+ peyar))

TE260i

{Z 57}

{C TOL}
vēṟṟumikkaṇ= um ataṉ ōraṟṟu* ē.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= īṟṟu+ peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}vēṟṟumikkaṇ= um ataṉ ōr aṟṟu{/T}
ukaravīṟṟu+ peyar vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarccikkaṇ= um av akaravīṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittu* āy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.

e-ṭu: {{kaṭukkāy; cetiḷ, tōl, pū}} eṉa varum.

((`eru' `ceru' eṉṉum coṟkaḷ))

TE261i

{Z 58}

{C TOL}
eru ~um^ ceru ~um ammoṭu civaṇi+
tiripiṭaṉ uṭiya teriyum^ kāli
ammiṉ makaram^ ceru vayiṉ keṭum ē
tam= oṟṟu mikūum valleḻuttu* iyaṟki.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki+ cāriyi viti ~um, oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉōṭu cāriyi viti ~um kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}eru ~um ceru ~um ammoṭu civaṇi+ tiripu iṭaṉ uṭiya teriyum kāli{/T}
eru eṉṉum col= um ceru eṉṉum col= um ammu+ cāriyiyoṭu porunti muṉ coṉṉa vēṟṟumi+ potuvitiyiṉ vēṟupaṭṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉ uṭiya ārāyuṅkālattu;
{T}ammiṉ makaram ceru vayiṉ keṭum{/T}
av= ammu+ cāriyiyatu īṟṟiṉ makaram ceru eṉṉum colliṭattu+ keṭṭu muṭiyum;
{T}valleḻuttu iyaṟki tam oṟṟu mikūum{/T}
avvāṟu keṭṭaviṭattu+ ceru eṉpatu valleḻuttu* ākiya iyalpi ~uṭiya tamatu oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum.

e-ṭu: {{eruvaṅkuḻi; cēṟu, tāṟu, pūḻi}} eṉa ~um; {{ceruvakkaḷam; cēṉi, tāṉi, paṟi}} eṉa ~um varum.

{T}teriyuṅkāli{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, eru eṉpataṟku+ perumpāṉmi melleḻuttu+ pēṟu* um, ciṟupāṉmi valleḻuttu+ pēṟu* um, ceru eṉpataṟku+ ciṟupāṉmi valleḻuttu+ pēṟu* um koḷka. {{eruṅkuḻi, erukkuḻi, cerukkaḷam}} eṉa varum.

{T}valleḻuttiyaṟki{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi av= iru moḻikku* um atikāra valleḻuttu vīḻvu* um koḷka. {{eruviṉkaṭumi, ceruviṉkaṭumi}} eṉa varum.

iṉṉum ataṉāṉ ē, {T}ammoṭu civaṇi+ tiripiṭaṉ uṭiya{/T} eṉa valleḻuttiṉkaṇṇatu* āka varintu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, ammu+ cāriyi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ peṟuvaṉa koḷka. {{eruvañāṟci, ceruvañāṟci}} eṉa varum.

avviṭattu ukaram vantu muṭika.

((7. ekara okara īṟu))

{C TOL}

((ekara okara iṭiccoṟkaḷ))

TE274i

{Z 71}

{C TOL}

mēl+ kūṟu* iyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, muṉ īṟu* ām eṉṉappaṭṭa ekara okara īṟṟu iṭiccoṟku* um av= īṟṟu muṉṉili viṉiccoṟku* um muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}tēṟṟa ekaram um ciṟappiṉ ov= um{/T}

{T}mēl kūṟu iyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā{/T}
mēli muṉṉili viṉiccoṟku+ kūṟappaṭum iyalpu* uṭiya valleḻuttu mikā ~āy iyalpu* āy muṭiyum.

{T}mēṟ kūṟu* iyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā{/T} eṉṟataṉāl "vantatu koṇṭu varātatu uṇarka" eṉṉum tantiravuttivakiyāṉ valleḻuttu mikum eṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭatu* āyiṟṟu.

e-ṭu: {{yāṉēe koṇṭēṉ, nīyēe koṇṭāy, avaṉēe koṇṭāṉ}} eṉa ~um; {{ (ōokoṇṭēṉ, ōokoṇṭāy,) ōo koṇṭāṉ}} eṉa ~um varum. ivi iṭiccol.

{{ēekkoṟṟā, ōokoṟṟā; cāttā, tēvā, pūtā}} eṉa ivi muṉṉiliviṉi. {T}iyaṟki{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, a+ muṉṉili viṉikaḷi aḷapeṭi ~āka niṟīikkoḷka.

((8. ēkāra īṟu))

((alvaḻiyil ēkāra īṟṟuppeyar))

TE275i

{Z 72}

{C TOL}

ōkāra ~īṟṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ= um av= ōkāravīṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu* ottu valleḻuttu+ peṟṟu+ puṇarum
{T}ā vayiṉ āṉa okaram varutal{/T}
avviṭattu okaram varuka.

e-ṭu: {{ōokkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi}} eṉa varum.

((`kō' muṉ `il'))

{C TOL}

{C URAI}

e-ṭu: {{kōyil}} eṉa varum.

((11. aukāra īṟu))

((iruvaḻiyil um aukāra īṟṟu mey))

{Z 93}

{C TOL}

valleḻuttu mikutal varinili ~iṉṟu* ē
av= iru īṟṟu* um ukaram varutal
cevvitu* eṉpa ciṟanticiṉōr ē.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, aukāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ= um vēṟṟumikkaṇ= um muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}aukāra iṟuti peyarnili muṉṉar alvaḻiyāṉ um vēṟṟumikkaṇ= um valleḻuttu mikutal varinili iṉṟu{/T}

e-ṭu: {{kuvukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu}} eṉa ~um; {{kuvukkaṭumi; ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi}} eṉa ~um varum.

`cevvitu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, meṉkaṇattukkaṇ= um iṭikkaṇattukkaṇ= um iruvaḻi ~um ukarappēṟu koḷka. {{kuvuñāṉṟatu, kuvuñāṟci}} eṉa ~um; {{kuvu valitu, kuvu valimi}} eṉa ~um varum.

e-ṭu: {{aḻakkuṭam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi}} eṉa varum; aḻaṉ eṉpatu, piṇam.

((`muṉ' eṉpataṟku muṉ `il'))

{Z 60}

{C TOL}

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= īṟṟuḷ ilakkaṇattōṭu poruntiya marūukkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{T}tol iyal maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapu{/T}
paḻiyatu* ākiya iyalpiṉi ~uṭiya ~iṭattu maruvivanta ilakkaṇa muṭipu.

e-ṭu: {{muṉṟil}} eṉa varum.

i+ colliṟku muṭipu* āka+ koḷa+ takum marapāṉ ē itaṉ muṭipu ākum iṭaṉ uṭittu.

{T}koḷattaku marapu{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, valleḻuttu mikkavaḻi aikāram varutal um, ākāram mikkavaḻi valleḻuttu mikutal um koḷka. iyalpu muṉ+ kūṟātu oḻintataṉāl, valleḻuttu mikuti ākāram vantavaḻi mikku* ē muṭitaliṉ ciṟappu* uṭittātal um, aikāram vantavaḻi mikutal um mikāmi ~um uṭimiyiṉ ciṟappu* iṉṟātal um koḷka.

e-ṭu: {{illikkal, illikal; ilkal; illākkal; cuṉi, tuṭi, paṟi}} eṉa varum.

{{illikkal, illikal}} eṉpaṉa illi eṉṉum aikāravīṟṟu+ col muṭipu* aṉṟu* ō ~eṉṟu* um, {{illākkal}} eṉpatu uḷḷa eṉṉum peyareccattu etirmaṟi ~ākiya ākāravīṟṟu+ collatu muṭipu* aṉṟu* ō ~eṉṟu* um, {{ilakal}} eṉpatu paṇputtoki muṭipu* aṉṟu* ō ~eṉṟu* um kūṟiṉ a+ muṭipukaḷōṭu i+ muṭipukaḷ eḻuttu* oppumi ~aṉṟi, ivi ōcivēṟṟumi ~uṭiya ~eṉpatu pōlum karuttu.

av= ōci vēṟṟumi ~āvaṉa, aikāravīṟu* āyavaḻi av= aikārattu mēl oliyūṉṟi ~um, lakāravīṟu* āyavaḻi anta lakāravoṟṟiṉ mēl oliyūṉṟi ~um, anta lakāravīṟu iyalpu muṭipu* āyavaḻi+ paṇputtoki pōla oru tiraṇmi ~āka oliyātu iṭiyaṟṟu lakārattu mēl oliyūṉṟi ~um, ākāravīṟu* āyavaḻi oru tiraṇmi ~āy av= ākārattu mēl oliyūṉṟi ~um, āṇṭu lakāravīṟu* āyavaḻi oru tiraṇmi ~āka oliyātu anta lakārattu mēl oliyūṉṟi ~um varum vēṟupāṭukaḷ pōlum.

il eṉpatu etirmaṟi viṉikkuṟippu-muṟṟu viravu-viṉi.

((`uṇṭu' eṉṉum col))

TE431i

{Z 25}

{C TOL}
uṇṭu* eṉ kiḷavi uṇmi ceppiṉ
munti iṟuti meyyoṭu* um^ keṭutal um
mēl* ^nili ~oṟṟu* ē ḷakāram ātal um
ā muṟi ~iraṇṭu* um urimi ~um uṭittu* ē
valleḻuttu varūum^ kāliyāṉa.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, meṉṟoṭar moḻiyuḷ oru viṉikkuṟippu moḻikku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}uṇṭu eṉ kiḷavi uṇmi ceppiṉ{/T}
uṇṭu eṉṉum col (uṇṭu eṉṉum toḻil oḻipa) uṇmi eṉṉum paṇpu uṇara niṟkum iṭattu
{T}munti iṟuti meyyoṭu* um keṭutal um mēl nili oṟṟu ḷakāram ātal um{/T}
muṟpaṭṭa īṟṟu+ kuṟṟiyalukaram (tāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa) meyyoṭu* um keṭutal um ataṟku mēl niṉṟa ṇakāra oṟṟu ḷakāram ātal um ākiya
{T}ā muṟi iraṇṭu* um urimi ~um uṭittu{/T}
a+ muṟimi ~uṭiya iraṇṭiṉi ~um urittākal um uṭittu
{T}valleḻuttu varuṅkāli{/T}
valleḻuttu mutal moḻi varuṅkālattu.

valleḻuttu* atikāram vārā niṟpa, (`valleḻuttu varūuṅkāli' eṉpataṉāṉ) iv iru muṭipu* um uḷḷatu. pakara mutal moḻi vantāl, maṟṟi mūṉṟu eḻuttiṉkaṇ= um īṟu keṭātu* ē niṉṟu muṭiyum eṉṟu koḷka.

iṉṉum ataṉāṉ ē, iyalpu kaṇattu iṟuti keṭātu* ē muṭital koḷka.

e-ṭu: {{uḷ poruḷ, uṇṭu poruḷ}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇṭu kāṇam, uṇṭu cākkāṭu, uṇṭu tāmari}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇṭuñāṇ; nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭi, āṭi}} eṉa ~um varum.

{{uḷporuḷ}} eṉpatu paṇputtoki muṭipu aṉṟu* ō eṉiṉ; aḵtu ōci oṟṟumippaṭa+ collum vaḻiyatu; iḵtu, ōci iṭiyaṟavupaṭa+ collum vaḻiyatu pōlum/.

{T}munti oṟṟu ḷakāram iraṭṭum{/T}
av= okarattiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa (ṉakara) oṟṟu iraṇṭu ḷakara oṟṟu* ām
{T}nūṟu eṉ kiḷavi nakāra mey keṭa ū ā ākum iyaṟkittu eṉpa{/T}
(varumoḻi ~ākiya) nūṟu eṉṉum col= um nakāram ākiya mey keṭa (ataṉ mēl ēṟiya) ūkāram ākāram ām iyalpi ~uṭittu eṉpar (pulavar)
{T}ā iṭi ikāram rakāram varutal{/T}
a+ moḻi ~iṭi ōr ikaram um rakāram um varum
{T}īṟu mey keṭuttu makāram oṟṟum{/T}

av= oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ eytiya muṭipu nili eyti muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.

eṉa ~ē, vaḻinili eṇ= ākiya iraṇṭu mutal ākiya eṇkaḷ a+ mutaṉili muṭipu* ākiya vikāram eyti ~um eytātu* um iyalpu* āy um muṭiyum.

e-ṭu: {{orukal; cuṉi, tuṭi, paṟi, ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu}} eṉa varum. {{irukal, iraṇṭukal; cuṉi, tuṭi, paṟi, ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu}} eṉa ~um, oṉpatiṉ kāṟum oṭṭuka.

{{oṉpatiṉkal}} eṉa+ ceṉṟatu* ēṉum vaḻakku* iṉmiyiṉ oḻikka.

`nili' eṉṟataṉāṉ, māṭṭēṟṟukku ēlāta ñakara-yakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṭipu koḷḷappaṭṭatu.

{INTRO} itu, iv ~atikārattu+ puṇarkkappaṭā moḻikaḷ ivi ~eṉa avaṟṟi eṭuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

{WFW}
{T}uyir um puḷḷi ~um iṟuti ~āki kuṟippiṉ um paṇpiṉ um iciyiṉ um tōṉṟi neṟippaṭa vārā kuṟiccol kiḷavi ~um{/T}
uyireḻuttu* um puḷḷiyeḻuttu* um īṟu* āki+ kuṟippiṉkaṇ= um paṇpiṉkaṇ= um iciyiṉkaṇ= um tōṉṟi oruneṟippaṭa vārā+ kuṟiccoṟkaḷ ākiya uriccoṟkaḷ um
{T}uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi ā iru maruṅkiṉ aimpāl aṟiyum paṇputtokumoḻi ~um{/T}
uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi ~ākiya av= iraṇṭu tiṇi ~iṭattu* um uḷavāya aintu pāliṉi ~um aṟiya varum paṇputtoki moḻikaḷ um
{T}ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum kiḷaviyiṉ mey oruṅku iyalum toḻil tokumoḻi ~um{/T}

{T}tam iyal kiḷappiṉ{/T}
(eṇkaḷ) tam iyalpu kiḷakkum iṭattu
{T}tam muṉ tām varum eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭa piṟa ~um aṉṉavi ellām{/T}
(niṟutta col= um kuṟittu varu kiḷavi ~um āy vārātu) tam muṉ tām varum eṇṇiṉ tokuti ~um uṭpaṭṭa piṟa ~um a+ taṉmiya ~ellām

vaḻakku* iṭattu maruvi naṭanta iṭattu* uḷḷaṉa

e-ṭu: {{viṇviṉittatu, kārkaṟuttatu, ollolittatu}}; ivi kuṟiccoṟkiḷavi āyiṉamiyiṉ muṭikkappaṭā ~āyiṉa; {{viṇṇeṉa vicittatu*}} eṉa iṭiccollōṭu kūṭiyavaḻi+ puṇarkkappaṭum.

{{karuñcāṉṟāṉ}} eṉṟatu paṇputtoki. itu karum eṉa+ paṇpu* uṇara niṉṟatu. {{karuñcāṉṟāṉ}} eṉa+ tokiyāyavaḻi, karum eṉpatu {{kariyāṉ}} eṉa+ pāl kāṭṭi niṟṟalil puṇarkkappaṭātu* āyiṟṟu.

{{kolyāṉi}} eṉṟatu viṉittoki. atu ~um kol= eṉa+ toḻiṉmi uṇaraniṉṟatu. {{kolyāṉi}} eṉa+ toki ~āyavaḻi, koṉṟa eṉa+ kālam kāṭṭi niṉṟamiyiṉ, puṇarkkappaṭātu* āyiṟṟu.

{{ōroṉṟu}} eṉa itu, {T}tam muṉ+ ^tām vanta eṇ{/T}. itu niṟutta col= um kuṟittuvarukiḷavi ~um aṇmiyiṟ puṇarkkappaṭātu* āyiṟṟu. itu tāṉ ē {{ōroṉṟu* āka+ koṭu}} eṉpuḻi+ puṇarkkappaṭum.

{T}piṟa ~um{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭiyātaṉa {{uṇṭāṉ, kariyaṉ}} eṉpaṉa. ivi {{uṇ}} eṉa ~um {{karumi}} eṉa ~um pirittu niṟuttiya vaḻi āṉ um aṉ= um kuṟittuvarukiḷavi ~aṉmiyāṉ vantu puṇarāmiyiṉ, puṇarkkappaṭā ~āyiṉa.

{T}tōṉṟi{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, {{koḷḷeṉa+ koṇṭāṉ}} eṉpuḻi+ koḷ= eṉpataṉi, eṉa eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarkkappaṭāmi koḷka.

{T}mey oruṅku* iyalum{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, {{uṇṭāṉ}} eṉpuḻi+ ceyki ~um kālam um pāl um tōṟṟi niṟkumāṟu pirittu+ puṇarkkappaṭāmi koḷka.
